<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center; background-color: white"><b>Explanatory Note</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">As previously reported
in the Current Report on Form 8-K filed by Texas Ventures Acquisition III Corp, a Cayman Islands exempted company limited by shares (the
&#8220;<b>Company</b>&#8221;), with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (the &#8220;<b>SEC</b>&#8221;) on October 20, 2026 (the
&#8220;<b>Initial 8-K</b>&#8221;), on October 19, 2026, the Company held an extraordinary general meeting of its shareholders (the &#8220;<b>Meeting</b>&#8221;),
at which the Company&#8217;s shareholders approved, by special resolution, an amendment to the Company&#8217;s amended and restated memorandum
and articles of association (the &#8220;<b>Extension Amendment</b>&#8221;) and an amendment to the Investment Management Trust Agreement,
dated April 22, 2025, by and between the Company and Continental Stock Transfer &amp; Trust Company (&#8220;<b>CST</b>&#8221;), as trustee,
to extend the date by which the Company must consummate an initial business combination and the related date on which CST must liquidate
the Company&#8217;s trust account (the &#8220;<b>Trust Account</b>&#8221;) established in connection with the Company&#8217;s initial
public offering that was consummated on April&#160;24, 2025 (the &#8220;<b>IPO</b>&#8221;), from October 24, 2026 to July 24, 2027.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">This Amendment No. 1
to the Initial 8-K (this &#8220;<b>Amendment</b>&#8221;) amends Item 7.01 of the Initial 8-K solely to correct the redemption price per
Class A ordinary share redeemed by the Company&#8217;s shareholders in connection with the Extension Amendment and the aggregate redemption
amount reported in connection with such redemptions. No other changes have been made to the Initial 8-K.</p>

<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0">&#160;</p>

<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top">
<td style="width: 10%"><b>Item&#160;8.01</b></td><td style="text-align: justify; width: 90%"><b>Other Events.</b></td></tr></table>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; background-color: white">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">In Item 7.01 of the Initial
8-K, we indicated based upon information provided to us by our transfer agent, CST, that in connection with the Extension Amendment, the
Company&#8217;s shareholders elected to redeem 17,699,488 Class A ordinary shares, which represents approximately 78.66% of the 22,500,000
Class A ordinary shares that were part of the units sold in the IPO, at a redemption price of approximately $10.58 per share, that such
shareholders redeemed approximately $187,260,583 from the Trust Account, and that following such redemptions, 4,800,512 Class A ordinary
shares remain outstanding.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white">CST has now provided
the Company with corrected information with respect to the redemption price. As a result, this Amendment amends the Initial 8-K to correct
that, in connection with the Extension Amendment, the Company&#8217;s shareholders elected to redeem 17,699,488 Class A ordinary shares
at a redemption price of approximately $10.63 per share, for an aggregate redemption amount of approximately $188,177,658.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; background-color: white">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; background-color: white"></p>
